# s5_opt_eta_results.txt -- plots/eta_sim/s5_opt_eta.C (2026-11-12): optimisation of the eta-selection cuts with the simulation truth
# SIMULATION ONLY (no measured data read; no combinatorial background exists in simulation and none is added).
# Simulation: SMASH std + lep + eta owned rows (mode A), w (PDG) x physics weights of the eta decays, main variant
#   simphys::kPhysMain = 108 (V3 direct decay + KLOE-2 bump + F_V conversion + background rules); std + lep + eta, mode A, w (PDG), phys 108
# sigma = sum w / L_sim, L_sim = 221.05 nb^-1; S, B = expected counts at L_data = 6080.5 nb^-1 (UNCONFIRMED, a scale only)
# base 'pre' = isBest >= 0, vz > -500, PT3 bit, vz > -160 mm, cor_ok == 1; all cut variables from corrected momenta
# windows (M4, [lo, hi)): PEAK 0.5333-0.5587, BUMP 0.4576-0.5255 GeV/c2
# targets: PEAK = classes 4+5 in the peak window; DIRECT = class 5 in the peak window (class 4 is background);
#          BUMP = classes 2+3 in the bump window; B = all other T1 + T2 rows of the window (variant '+T3T4': + templates 3 + 4)
# FOM F = S / sqrt(S + B); purity S / (S + B); efficiency S / S_pre. Errors: simulation statistics, event-group variance
#   (rows of one event group correlated), delta method for F. X17 rows (class 6) never enter.
# rows: 8555101 compact pre rows owned in mode A (X17 dropped 787659, w <= 0 dropped 1035391); window rows 848479; true-angle lookups hit 3252056 miss 0
# kept rows per set std 115414 lep 7082339 eta 1357348; w <= 0 rows per set std 0 lep 0 eta 1035391, per template T1 0 T2 996891 T3 20140 T4 18360
#   (w <= 0: eta-set rows whose only forced decay is X17 carry W = 2 BR(X17) = 0, see SimEngine.h)

## 0. Method
# Exhaustive grid search, exact at the grid values: every window row gets per variable the index of the grid interval of its
# value (edges = cut values, float comparison as the SimEngine presets: x > a for lower cuts, x < b for upper cuts, NaN fails
# every real cut); weights are filled into a 9-D histogram (7 quantities x 2 halves, double) and summed cumulatively along each
# axis (suffix sums for lower cuts and flags, prefix sums for upper cuts), so each cell holds S and B of one cut combination.
# Cells: 13824000. Plateau = F >= 0.95 F_best. Profile = best F over all other variables at a fixed value; slice = F with the other
# variables at the chosen point.
# Freeze rule: among cells with F >= 0.95 F_best on the full sample AND on each half (relative to that half's best), the fewest
# active cuts; among those the highest worst-neighbour F (each active threshold moved one grid step; flags have no neighbour),
# required >= 0.90 F_best (else one more cut is allowed); ties by F.
# Split: half A = event groups with an even ordinal grp (per tree = per set; 'fold' column), half B = odd; each half x 2.
# Bias = F(optimising half) - F(other half) at the cells chosen on the optimising half (best cell, and the freeze rule applied
# to that half alone). Random splits: half = bit 0 of splitmix64((set << 40) ^ grp ^ (seed << 48)), seeds 1..100.
# grids (none = no cut; flags off/on):
#   oa_min               x >   : none 1 1.5 2 2.5 3 4 5 6 8  [deg]
#   alpha_lab_max        x <   : 25 30 35 40 45 50 55 60 70 none  [deg]
#   mpipi_min            x >   : none 0.28 0.3 0.32 0.34 0.36 0.38 0.4 0.42  [GeV/c^{2}]
#   mpipi_max            x <   : 0.38 0.4 0.41 0.42 0.44 0.46 0.48 0.5 0.53 none  [GeV/c^{2}]
#   alpha_eta_min        x >   : none 100 120 130 140 150 160 170  [deg]
#   pions_in_vertex_fit  flag  : off on  []
#   dz_pi_lt_40          flag  : off on  []
#   mm4_max              x <   : 2.5 2.6 2.7 2.75 2.8 2.85 2.9 none  [GeV/c^{2}]
#   pt4_min              x >   : none 100 200 300 400 500  [MeV/c]
